DATABASES KNOWLEDGE BASE

Entry 1
Category: Database Fundamentals
Concept: Database
Definition: An organized collection of related data.
Key Points:
- Core concept in database systems.
- Frequently covered in DBMS courses and technical interviews.
- Widely used in software development and data management.
Example:
Database is commonly used in modern database systems.
Related Concepts:
SQL, Tables, Transactions, Indexing, Normalization, NoSQL

Example:

Entry 18
Category: SQL
Concept: GROUP BY

Key Points:
- Core concept in database systems.
- Frequently covered in DBMS courses and technical interviews.
- Widely used in software development and data management.
Example:
SELECT Department, COUNT(*) FROM Employees GROUP BY Department;

SQL, Tables, Transactions, Indexing, Normalization, NoSQL

Entry 20
Category: SQL
Concept: JOIN
Definition: Combines rows from multiple tables.
Key Points:
- Core concept in database systems.
- Frequently covered in DBMS courses and technical interviews.
- Widely used in software development and data management.
Example:
SELECT * FROM Students INNER JOIN Courses ON Students.CourseID = Courses.ID;
Related Concepts:
SQL, Tables, Transactions, Indexing, Normalization, NoSQL
